- **pubdev-api-client**: pubdev_viewer の API クライアント層・エラーハンドリング・Repository パターン。 Dio ベースの ApiClient 設計、DioException → sealed AppException 変換、 PubDevApiClient の継承パターン、keepAlive Provider、Repository の fromJson パースを 実装・編集する際に使用。「API を追加」「エンドポイント追加」「Dio」「エラーハンドリング」 「Repository を作って」「AppException」「ネットワークエラー」と言われたときに参照。 新しい feature の API 接続・例外処理を書く場面では必ずこのスキルを確認すること。 Fake の統一パターン自体は /pubdev-testing が扱う。 ([YukiKitaike/pubdev_viewer](https://github.com/YukiKitaike/pubdev_viewer/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pubdev-api-client))
- **pubdev-models**: pubdev_viewer の Freezed + json_serializable モデル定義パターンを提供する。 API Response モデル（fromJson あり）と State モデル（fromJson なし）の クラス構造・@JsonKey・DateTime コンバーター・core/models/ 昇格基準を扱う。 「モデルを作って」「Freezed」「fromJson」「JsonKey」と言われたときに参照。 State を扱う Notifier 側の実装（loadMore 等）は /pubdev-state が扱う。 ([YukiKitaike/pubdev_viewer](https://github.com/YukiKitaike/pubdev_viewer/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pubdev-models))
- **pubdev-navigation**: pubdev_viewer の GoRouter + go_router_builder による型安全ルーティング。 新しい画面のルート追加・画面遷移の実装時に使用。 「ルートを追加」「画面遷移」「ナビゲーション」「GoRoute」と言われたときに参照。 TypedGoRoute・GoRouteData・パスパラメータ・ネストルートのパターンを提供する。 ([YukiKitaike/pubdev_viewer](https://github.com/YukiKitaike/pubdev_viewer/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pubdev-navigation))
- **pubdev-new-feature**: pubdev_viewer に新しい feature を縦切りで追加するステップバイステップガイド。 models → repository → notifier → screen → route → test の全レイヤーを網羅する。 /pubdev-new-feature &lt;feature名&gt; で明示的に呼び出して使用する。 単一レイヤーのみの作業（既存画面の UI 修正・モデル追加等）は 各ドメインスキル（/pubdev-ui, /pubdev-models 等）を直接使用する。 ([YukiKitaike/pubdev_viewer](https://github.com/YukiKitaike/pubdev_viewer/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pubdev-new-feature))

- **public-02-claude-skills-main-agent-launcher-commands-cs-grade-f9479ed1**: Phase 3 — the bounded grade→iterate loop. Never unbounded. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-agent-launcher-commands-cs-grade-f9479ed1))
- **public-02-claude-skills-main-agent-launcher-commands-cs-grill-agent-launcher-18ed1a35**: Matt Pocock docs-anchored grill for an agent-launcher goal — walks the phase's forcing questions ONE at a time, each with a recommended answer and a citation to a reference doc, refusing to advance on a fuzzy input. Use to pressure-test a CMA plan before launching. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-agent-launcher-commands-cs-grill-agent-launcher-18ed1a35))
- **public-02-claude-skills-main-agent-launcher-commands-cs-interview-b04091d7**: Phase 1 — interview the founder into a validated CMA build sheet (primitives table + v1/v2 deferrals + eval plan) via the interview skill. No API key needed. Walks the six intake slots, maps to primitives, validates against CMA limits. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-agent-launcher-commands-cs-interview-b04091d7))
- **public-02-claude-skills-main-agent-launcher-commands-cs-launch-b01d6b4e**: Main entry / resume for building a Claude Managed Agent. Runs the agent-launcher-orchestrator skill from the current session goal — reads ./my-agent/goal.json, routes to the right phase (interview → stage-launch → grade-iterate → run-without-you → wrap-up), compiles the loop/workflow, and forks to the phase skill. Emits BYOK curl; never makes API calls or prints the key. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-agent-launcher-commands-cs-launch-b01d6b4e))
- **public-02-claude-skills-main-agent-launcher-commands-cs-run-without-you-3900d9ca**: Phase 4 — make the agent run without you. Turn a graded agent into a recurring POSIX-cron scheduled deployment (optionally self-grading each firing), an event-driven curl trigger, or on-demand use, then finalize NEXT-DIRECTIONS, via the run-without-you skill. Tests with a manual run before trusting the schedule. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-agent-launcher-commands-cs-run-without-you-3900d9ca))
- **public-02-claude-skills-main-agent-launcher-commands-cs-stage-launch-b4bbb20b**: Phase 2 — turn a build sheet into exact API payloads and a resumable BYOK curl launch script, then launch (environment → agent → session → kickoff) with the founder's own key via the stage-launch skill. No tool makes API calls; the key never enters chat. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-agent-launcher-commands-cs-stage-launch-b4bbb20b))
- **public-02-claude-skills-main-agent-launcher-commands-cs-wrap-up-a7f5d562**: Close out a launched Claude Managed Agent — recap every primitive owned, regenerate the single-file overview page, and suggest the next 1–2 upgrades, via the wrap-up skill. This reference enumerates the eight most common failure modes with sources and the specific guard each tool implements. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-business-operations-skills-capacity-planner-references-capacity-anti-patterns-02fa0081))

The other half is the human reality of hiring, ramping, retaining, and structuring the people who staff the queue. This reference assembles the operational-leadership canon needed to translate an Erlang-C number into an executable 12-month plan. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-business-operations-skills-capacity-planner-references-ops-workforce-planning-canon-c5c5b16b))
- **public-02-claude-skills-main-business-operations-skills-capacity-planner-references-queueing-theory-canon-7b3647b4**: Capacity planning for ops teams (Support, CX, BizOps, Finance ops, IT ops) without queueing theory is guesswork. The fundamental insight: as utilization approaches 100%, wait time approaches infinity non-linearly. Plan to 90% and your SLA collapses. Plan to 70-80% and you have surge capacity. The math is over 100 years old, and ignoring it is the most expensive mistake an ops leader makes. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-02-claude-skills-main-business-operations-skills-capacity-planner-references-queueing-theory-canon-7b3647b4))
